## 4.5 以下例题（**Practice Questions**）

| **1.What is the payoff for a long call option at expiration if the underlying price S\_T is greater than the strike X?**<br />**1. 如果标的价格 S\_T 大于行权价 X，看涨期权买方在到期时的收益是多少？**<br />A) Max(0, X - S\_T)<br />A) Max(0, X - S\_T)<br />B) Max(0, S\_T - X)<br />B) Max(0, S\_T - X)<br />C) Premium paid<br />C) 已付权利金 | **B**<br />**Explanation:** The payoff for a long call is max(0, S\_T - X), as it profits from the difference when S\_T > X minus the premium.<br />**解释：** 看涨期权买方的收益是 max(0, S\_T - X)，因为当 S\_T > X 时，它从差价中获利（减去权利金）。 |
| ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |

| **2.For a short put seller, what is the maximum risk?**<br />**2. 对于看跌期权卖方，最大风险是多少？**<br />A) Unlimited<br />A) 无限<br />B) Limited to the premium received<br />B) 限于收到的权利金<br />C) Limited to X - premium (down to zero)<br />C) 限于 X - 权利金（直至归零） | **C**<br />**Explanation:** Short put risk is limited to X - premium, as the seller must buy at X if exercised, with the underlying potentially going to zero.<br />**解释：** 卖出看跌期权的风险限于 X - 权利金，因为如果行权，卖方必须以 X 买入，而标的资产可能归零。 |
| --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |

| **3.If an option has positive intrinsic value for a call, it is classified as:**<br />**3. 如果看涨期权具有正的内在价值，它被分类为：**<br />A) Out-of-the-Money (OTM)<br />A) 虚值 (OTM)<br />B) At-the-Money (ATM)<br />B) 平值 (ATM)<br />C) In-the-Money (ITM)<br />C) 实值 (ITM) | **C**<br />**Explanation:** ITM for calls means S > X, with positive intrinsic value (S - X).<br />**解释：** 看涨期权的 ITM 意味着 S > X，具有正的内在价值 (S - X)。 |
| --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |

| **4.What does option time value represent?**<br />**4. 期权时间价值代表什么？**<br />A) The immediate exercise value<br />A) 立即行权价值<br />B) The extra premium for potential favorable moves before expiration<br />B) 为到期前潜在有利变动支付的额外权利金<br />C) The risk-free interest rate<br />C) 无风险利率 | **B**<br />**Explanation:** Time value is the premium beyond intrinsic value, paying for the chance of price movement over time.<br />**解释：** 时间价值是超出内在价值的权利金，为随时间推移的价格变动机会付费。 |
| ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |

| **5. What does a call option give the buyer the right to do?**<br />**5. 看涨期权赋予买方什么权利？**<br />A) Sell the underlying asset<br />A) 卖出标的资产<br />B) Buy the underlying asset<br />B) 买入标的资产<br />C) Hold the underlying asset<br />C) 持有标的资产 | **B**<br />**Explanation:** A call option grants the buyer the right, but not the obligation, to buy the underlying asset at the strike price.<br />**解释：** 看涨期权赋予买方以行权价买入标的资产的权利，但非义务。 |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |

| **6.American options can be exercised:**<br />**6. 美式期权可以在何时行权？**<br />A) Only at expiration<br />A) 仅在到期时<br />B) Anytime before or on expiration<br />B) 到期前或到期时的任何时间<br />C) Only on specific dates<br />C) 仅在特定日期 | **B**<br />**Explanation: **American options allow exercise at any time up to expiration, unlike European ones.<br />**解释：** 与欧式期权不同，美式期权允许在到期前的任何时间行权。 |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |

| **7. For a long call, the breakeven point is:**<br />**7. 对于看涨期权买方，盈亏平衡点是：**<br />A) Strike - Premium<br />A) 行权价 - 权利金<br />B) Strike + Premium<br />B) 行权价 + 权利金<br />C) Strike only<br />C) 仅行权价 | **B**<br />**Explanation:** The breakeven for a long call is the strike price plus the premium paid, where the payoff offsets the cost.<br />**解释：** 看涨期权买方的盈亏平衡点是行权价加上已付权利金，此时收益抵消了成本。 |
| --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |

| **8.For a long put buyer, the loss is limited to:**<br />**8. 对于看跌期权买方，损失限于：**<br />A) Unlimited downside<br />A) 无限下行<br />B) The premium paid<br />B) 已付权利金<br />C) X - S\_T<br />C) X - S\_T | **B**<br />**Explanation:** Long put maximum loss is the premium if the option expires worthless.<br />**解释：** 如果期权过期作废，看跌期权买方的最大损失是权利金。 |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |

| **9. What is intrinsic value for a call option?**<br />**9. 看涨期权的内在价值是什么？**<br />A) Strike - Stock price (if positive)<br />A) 行权价 - 股价（如果为正）<br />B) Stock price - Strike (if positive)<br />B) 股价 - 行权价（如果为正）<br />C) Time value only<br />C) 仅时间价值 | **B**<br />**Explanation:** Intrinsic value for a call is the current stock price minus the strike if positive; otherwise, it's zero.<br />**解释：** 看涨期权的内在价值是当前股价减去行权价（如果为正）；否则为零。 |
| --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |

| **10.An option is in-the-money (ITM) for a put if:**<br />**10. 如果看跌期权是实值 (ITM)，则：**<br />A) Stock price > Strike<br />A) 股价 > 行权价<br />B) Stock price < Strike<br />B) 股价 < 行权价<br />C) Stock price = Strike<br />C) 股价 = 行权价 | **B**<br />**Explanation: **A put is ITM when the stock price is below the strike, giving it positive intrinsic value<br />**解释：** 当股价低于行权价时，看跌期权为 ITM，赋予其正的内在价值 |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |

| **11.What is the expiration time for Zero Days to Expiration (0DTE) options?**<br />**11. 末日期权 (0DTE) 的到期时间是什么？**<br />A) At the end of the trading week<br />A) 在交易周结束时<br />B) At the end of the same trading day<br />B) 在同一交易日结束时<br />C) One month after purchase<br />C) 购买后一个月 | **B**<br />**Explanation:** 0DTE options expire at the end of the same trading day they are purchased or sold.<br />**解释：** 0DTE 期权在买入或卖出的同一交易日结束时到期。 |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |

| **12.For a 0DTE call option, what happens if it is out-of-the-money (OTM) at close?**<br />**12. 对于 0DTE 看涨期权，如果在收盘时是虚值 (OTM)，会发生什么？**<br />A) It has unlimited profit<br />A) 它有无限利润<br />B) It expires worthless<br />B) 它过期作废<br />C) It is automatically exercised<br />C) 它被自动行权 | **B**<br />**Explanation:** If a 0DTE call is ATM/OTM (S\_T ≤ X), it expires worthless, and the full premium is lost.<br />**解释：** 如果 0DTE 看涨期权是 ATM/OTM (S\_T ≤ X)，它将过期作废，损失全部权利金。 |
| ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
# 5. 基金（Fund）

> 考试小贴士：本章节题目占比为10%左右。
>
> * 5.1-5.4部分为纯概念考察。
>
> * 5.5-5.7部分为重点考察对象，需要查概念+灵活运用。你需要了解有哪些不同种类的基金投资工具和他们的异同之处（5.5）、不同类型基金的特点以及他们各自的投资目标（5.6）以及基金有哪些主要的费用（5.7）。
>
> * 产品层面：如何在MooMoo App中找到不同类型的基金并且购买、如何在App中查看和对比不同基金的信息以及费率。

**Introduction to Wealth Management Products**
**财富管理产品介绍**

This training material provides a basic overview of key investment vehicles in the US market: money market funds, mutual funds, hybrid (or balanced) funds, and private markets (including private equity and venture capital). The content is primarily based on the CFA Level 1 Curriculum, which covers aspects of the asset management industry and pooled investment products.
本培训材料提供了美国市场主要投资工具的基本概述：货币市场基金、共同基金、混合（或平衡）基金以及私募市场（包括私募股权和风险投资）。内容主要基于 CFA 一级课程，涵盖资产管理行业和集合投资产品的各个方面。

## 5.1 **资产管理行业概述 Overview of the Asset Management Industry**

The asset management industry manages investments for individuals and institutions, helping them grow wealth while managing risk. As of 2017 (from the curriculum), global AUM was over $79 trillion, with nearly 80% in North America and Europe. By 2025, this has grown to about $110 trillion globally, with the US alone accounting for around $50 trillion.
资产管理行业为个人和机构管理投资，帮助他们在管理风险的同时实现财富增值。截至 2017 年（来自课程），全球资产管理规模 (AUM) 超过 79 万亿美元，其中近 80% 在北美和欧洲。到 2025 年，全球资产管理规模已增长至约 110 万亿美元，仅美国就占约 50 万亿美元。

Key aspects from the curriculum:
课程中的关键点：

* **Industry Structure**: Includes "pure-play" asset managers (e.g., Vanguard, BlackRock) and diversified firms like banks or insurers that offer investment services.
* **行业结构**：包括“纯粹”的资产管理公司（如 Vanguard、BlackRock）以及提供投资服务的银行或保险公司等多元化公司。

* **Global Reach**: Many firms have offices worldwide to handle international investments.
* **全球覆盖**：许多公司在全球设有办事处以处理国际投资。

* **Competition**: The industry is highly competitive, focusing on performance, fees, and client service.
* **竞争**：行业竞争激烈，专注于业绩、费用和客户服务。

* **Supplement for Novices**: Asset managers charge fees (e.g., 0.5%-1% of AUM annually) for their services. In the US, they must register with the SEC if managing over $100 million in assets, ensuring transparency and investor protection.
* **新手补充**：资产管理公司对其服务收取费用（例如每年 AUM 的 0.5%-1%）。在美国，如果管理资产超过 1 亿美元，必须向 SEC 注册，以确保透明度和投资者保护。

## 5.2 **主动与被动管理 Active vs. Passive Management**

The curriculum distinguishes two main approaches to managing investments:
课程区分了两种主要的投资管理方法：

* **Active Management**: Managers actively select securities to outperform a benchmark (e.g., S\&P 500). They use research, forecasts, and timing to buy/sell. This is common in mutual funds aiming for higher returns but involves higher fees and risks if the manager underperforms.
* **主动管理**：管理者主动选择证券以跑赢基准（如标准普尔 500 指数）。他们利用研究、预测和择时进行买卖。这在旨在获得更高回报的共同基金中很常见，但也涉及更高的费用，如果管理者表现不佳，风险也更大。

* **Passive Management**: Managers track a market index (e.g., via ETFs like SPY). Lower fees, as there's no "picking" involved. The curriculum notes passive strategies have grown due to cost efficiency.
* **被动管理**：管理者追踪市场指数（例如通过 SPY 等 ETF）。由于不涉及“选股”，费用较低。课程指出，由于成本效益，被动策略已经增长。

* **Supplement for Novices**: In the US, passive funds now hold over 50% of equity fund assets, active management can beat the market in volatile times but often lags after fees (source: Morningstar Active/Passive Barometer 2025). For clients, recommend passive for low-cost diversification and active for specialized goals.
* **新手补充**：在美国，被动基金目前持有超过 50% 的股票基金资产，主动管理在波动时期可能跑赢大盘，但在扣除费用后往往落后（来源：晨星主动/被动晴雨表 2025）。对于客户，建议使用被动基金进行低成本多元化配置，使用主动基金实现特定目标。

## 5.3 **传统资产管理公司与另类资产管理公司 Traditional vs. Alternative Asset Managers**

* **Traditional Managers**: Focus on stocks, bonds, and cash. They manage mutual funds and aim for steady growth.
* **传统管理者**：专注于股票、债券和现金。他们管理共同基金，旨在实现稳定增长。

* **Alternative Managers**: Handle non-traditional assets like hedge funds, private equity, real estate, or commodities. These often seek higher returns but with more risk and less liquidity.
* **另类管理者**：处理对冲基金、私募股权、房地产或大宗商品等非传统资产。这些通常寻求更高的回报，但也伴随着更高的风险和更低的流动性。

* **Insight**: Alternatives have grown due to low interest rates and the search for yield. In 2017, alternatives were about 15% of global AUM; by 2025, they've risen to 20%&#x20;
* **洞察**：由于低利率和对收益率的追寻，另类资产有所增长。2017 年，另类资产约占全球 AUM 的 15%；到 2025 年，这一比例已升至 20%。

* **Supplement for Novices**: In the US, alternatives are for "accredited investors" (net worth >$1 million or income >$200k), per SEC rules, due to higher risks (source: SEC Regulation D).
* **新手补充**：在美国，根据 SEC 规则（SEC Regulation D），由于风险较高，另类资产面向“合格投资者”（净资产 > 100 万美元或收入 > 20 万美元）。

## 5.4 **资产管理行业趋势 Asset Management Industry Trends**

**Key trends:**
**主要趋势：**

* 1.Shift to passive investing.
* 1.转向被动投资。

* 2.Growth in alternatives for diversification.
* 2.另类资产增长以实现多元化。

* 3.Technology (e.g., robo-advisors) lowering costs.
* 3.技术（如智能投顾）降低成本。

* 4.Focus on ESG (environmental, social, governance) factors.
* 4.关注 ESG（环境、社会、治理）因素。

## 5.5 **集合投资工具 Pooled Investment Vehicles**

### 共同基金 Mutual Funds (General Operations)

Mutual funds pool money from many investors to buy a diversified portfolio of securities. They are regulated under the Investment Company Act of 1940 in the US.
共同基金汇集多位投资者的资金，购买多元化的证券组合。它们在美国受 1940 年投资公司法监管。

**Operations:**
**运作：**

* **Open-end:** Investors buy/redeem shares directly from the fund at net asset value (NAV), calculated daily (closing prices of holdings minus liabilities, divided by shares). Funds can grow by issuing new shares. Redemptions may require selling assets, so funds hold some cash for liquidity.
* **开放式**：投资者按资产净值 (NAV) 直接向基金购买/赎回份额，NAV 每日计算（持仓收盘价减去负债，除以份额）。基金可以通过发行新份额来增长。赎回可能需要出售资产，因此基金持有一些现金以保持流动性。

| Open-ended Fund Example:MooMoo App——更多——理财<br />开放式基金示例：MooMoo App——更多——理财 | ![](<images/入门考试学习资料 Learning Resources for Entry Knowledge Test-image1.PNG>) |
| ------------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- |

* **Closed end:** Fixed shares, traded on exchanges at market price (may be premium/discount to NAV). No new shares issued after IPO, limited growth but no liquidity pressure.&#x20;
* **封闭式**：份额固定，在交易所按市场价格交易（可能有溢价/折价）。IPO 后不再发行新份额，增长有限但没有流动性压力。

* **Fees:** Expense ratio (0.4% average in 2025); load (sales charge) or no-load. Active funds have higher fees for research.&#x20;
* **费用**：费用率（2025 年平均为 0.4%）；佣金（销售费用）或免佣。主动型基金因研究成本而收费较高。

* **Taxes:** Pass-through; capital gains distributed annually, potentially taxable.
* **税务**：穿透式；资本收益每年分配，可能需要纳税。

| Close-ended Fund Example:<br />封闭式基金示例： | ![](<images/入门考试学习资料 Learning Resources for Entry Knowledge Test-image-78.png>) |
| ------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |

**Investment Points:**
**投资要点：**

* **Benefits:** Diversification, professional management, good liquidity, low minimums.
* **优势**：多元化、专业管理、良好的流动性、低门槛。

* **Risks:** Market risk, fees reduce returns, potential for underperformance vs. benchmarks.
* **风险**：市场风险、费用降低回报、可能跑输基准。

* **Suitability:** Great for beginners; passive for cost-conscious, active for potential outperformance.
* **适用性**：非常适合初学者；被动型适合注重成本者，主动型适合追求跑赢大盘者。

* **Example:** Vanguard 500 Index Fund (VFIAX) – Passive, tracks S\&P 500. AUM around $1 trillion in 2025; 2025 return around 17.7% (matching large-cap index).
* **示例**：Vanguard 500 Index Fund (VFIAX) – 被动型，追踪标准普尔 500 指数。2025 年 AUM 约为 1 万亿美元；2025 年回报率约为 17.7%（匹配大盘指数）。

**Compare: Open-ended funds, closed ended funds and ETFs:**
**比较：开放式基金、封闭式基金和 ETF：**

| **Aspect**<br>**方面** | **Open-Ended Funds**<br>**开放式基金** | **Closed-Ended Funds**<br>**封闭式基金** | **Exchange-Traded Funds (ETFs)**<br>**交易所交易基金 (ETF)** |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| Structure<br>结构 | Comingled investment pool: new shares issued for inflows, redeemed for outflows. Unlimited shares.<br>混合投资池：资金流入发行新份额，流出赎回份额。份额无限。 | Comingled investment pool; fixed number of shares after initial offering. No new shares issued.<br>混合投资池；首次发行后份额固定。不发行新份额。 | Generally structured as open-end funds but trade on exchanges like stocks. Shares created/redeemed in large blocks by authorized participants.<br>通常结构为开放式基金，但在交易所像股票一样交易。份额由授权参与者大宗创建/赎回。 |
| Trading<br>交易 | Bought/sold directly from the fund.<br>直接向基金买卖。 | Traded on exchanges intraday between investors.<br>在投资者之间在交易所进行日内交易。 | Traded on exchanges intraday between investors, similar to stocks. Can be short-sold or bought on margin.<br>像股票一样在投资者之间在交易所进行日内交易。可以卖空或融资买入。 |
| Pricing<br>定价 | At net asset value (NAV), calculated daily based on underlying assets.<br>按资产净值 (NAV)，基于标的资产每日计算。 | At market price, which can be at a premium or discount to NAV.<br>按市场价格，可能相对于 NAV 有溢价或折价。 | At market price, which typically stays close to NAV due to arbitrage by authorized participants.<br>按市场价格，由于授权参与者的套利，通常保持接近 NAV。 |
| Liquidity<br>流动性 | High; redeem shares at NAV (may require selling assets to meet redemptions).<br>高；按 NAV 赎回份额（可能需要出售资产以满足赎回）。 | Depends on market demand; sell to other investors (may be less liquid if trading volume is low).<br>取决于市场需求；卖给其他投资者（如果交易量低，流动性可能较差）。 | High; intraday trading, but underlying liquidity depends on assets. Dividends paid out (not reinvested).<br>高；日内交易，但底层流动性取决于资产。股息派发（不自动再投资）。 |
| Fees<br>费用 | Management fee (annual % of AUM); may include load fees (sales charge) or no-load. Higher for active management.<br>管理费（AUM 的年度百分比）；可能包括佣金（销售费用）或免佣。主动管理费用较高。 | Management fee; may trade at discount/premium, no load on secondary market trades.<br>管理费；可能折价/溢价交易，二级市场交易无佣金。 | Low management fees (often lower than mutual funds); trading commissions may apply, but no load fees.<br>低管理费（通常低于共同基金）；可能适用交易佣金，但无销售费用。 |
| Minimum Investment<br>最低投资额 | Varies, but often higher than ETFs (e.g., $1,000+).<br>各异，但通常高于 ETF（例如 $1,000+）。 | Varies by fund, bought on exchange like stocks.<br>因基金而异，像股票一样在交易所购买。 | Low; can buy as few as one share.<br>低；最少可买一股。 |
| Advantages<br>优势 | Easy growth in size; daily liquidity at NAV; diversified with professional management.<br>规模易于增长；按 NAV 的每日流动性；专业管理的多元化。 | No need to manage inflows/outflows; no liquidity pressure from redemptions.<br>无需管理资金流入/流出；没有来自赎回的流动性压力。 | Intraday trading; tax efficiency; low costs; diversification; ability to short/margin.<br>日内交易；税务效率；低成本；多元化；可卖空/融资。 |
| Disadvantages<br>劣势 | Liquidity management (must hold cash for redemptions); potential capital gains distributions from trading.<br>流动性管理（必须持有现金以备赎回）；交易可能产生资本收益分配。 | Can trade at premium/discount; limited growth potential.<br>可能溢价/折价交易；增长潜力有限。 | Market price may deviate slightly from NAV; dividends not reinvested automatically.<br>市场价格可能略微偏离 NAV；股息不自动再投资。 |
| Examples from Curriculum<br>课程示例 | Most mutual funds (e.g., stock, bond, money market funds).<br>大多数共同基金（如股票、债券、货币市场基金）。 | Represent about 1% of US mutual fund assets.<br>约占美国共同基金资产的 1%。 | Fast-growing; often passive/index based.<br>快速增长；通常是被动/基于指数的。 |

## 5.6 **不同类型的基金 Different Types of Fund**

### 5.6.1 货币市场基金 Money Market Funds

**Operations**: Invest in short-term (under 90 days), low-risk debt like Treasury bills, commercial paper. Aim for stable $1 NAV (CNAV) or variable (VNAV). Taxable (corporate/gov't debt) or tax-free (muni debt). Daily liquidity; Not FDIC-insured but considered safe. The yield on the U.S. dollar money market funds generally tracks the short-term T-bill rates closely, which means the yield on money market funds generally goes up in the rate hike cycle and goes down during the cut cycle.
**运作**：投资于短期（90 天以内）、低风险债务，如国库券、商业票据。旨在保持稳定的 1 美元 NAV (CNAV) 或可变 NAV (VNAV)。应税（公司/政府债务）或免税（市政债务）。每日流动性；没有 FDIC 保险但被认为是安全的。美元货币市场基金的收益率通常紧密追踪短期国库券利率，这意味着货币市场基金的收益率通常在加息周期中上升，在降息周期中下降。

| 3-Month T-Bill Yield and Money Market Funds yield:<br />3个月期国库券收益率与货币市场基金收益率： | ![](<images/入门考试学习资料 Learning Resources for Entry Knowledge Test-image3-1.png>) |
| -------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |

| Money Market Funds Example: MooMoo App——理财——cash plus（现金管理）<br />货币市场基金示例：MooMoo App——理财——cash plus（现金管理） | ![](<images/入门考试学习资料 Learning Resources for Entry Knowledge Test-image4.PNG>) |
| ----------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- |

**Investment Points:**
**投资要点：**

* **Benefits:** Principal preservation, high liquidity, yields \~ money market rates (e.g., 4-5% in 2025 amid high rates).
* **优势**：本金保值、高流动性、收益率接近货币市场利率（例如在 2025 年高利率环境下为 4-5%）。

* **Risks:** Low returns, inflation risk; rare "breaking the buck" (NAV < $1, as in 2008 crisis, leading to reforms).
* **风险**：低回报、通胀风险；罕见的“跌破面值”（NAV < $1，如 2008 年危机，导致改革）。

* **Suitability:** For cash reserves, short-term needs; conservative clients.
* **适用性**：用于现金储备、短期需求；保守型客户。

* **Example:** Fidelity Government Money Market Fund (SPAXX) – Taxable, invests in US gov't securities. AUM around $500B+ in 2025; yields around 4.5% in late 2025.
* **示例**：Fidelity Government Money Market Fund (SPAXX) – 应税，投资于美国政府证券。2025 年 AUM 约为 5000 亿美元以上；2025 年底收益率约为 4.5%。

### 5.6.2 混合型/平衡型基金 Hybrid/Balanced Funds

**Operations:**
**运作：**

A balanced fund is a combination of a growth fund and an income fund. Balanced funds invest in common stocks, preferred stocks, long-term bonds, and short-term bonds, aiming to provide both income and capital appreciation while minimizing risk. These funds don’t get hammered too badly when the market is bearish but usually underperform when the market is bullish.&#x20;
平衡型基金是增长型基金和收益型基金的结合。平衡型基金投资于普通股、优先股、长期债券和短期债券，旨在提供收入和资本增值的同时最大限度地降低风险。这些基金在市场看跌时不会受到太大的打击，但在市场看涨时通常表现不佳。

**Investment Points:**
**投资要点：**

* **Benefits:** Built-in diversification, moderate risk/return; automatic rebalancing.
* **优势**：内置多元化、中等风险/回报；自动再平衡。

* **Risks:** Equity volatility, interest rate sensitivity; fees higher than pure index funds.
* **风险**：股票波动、利率敏感性；费用高于纯指数基金。

* **Suitability:** Moderate-risk investors, retirement planning; simplifies allocation.
* **适用性**：中等风险投资者、退休计划；简化配置。

* **Example:** Vanguard Balanced Index Fund (VBALX) – 60% stocks/40% bonds. AUM \~$50B in 2025; historical 7-8% annual returns, 2025 return \~12% amid stock gains.
* **示例**：Vanguard Balanced Index Fund (VBALX) – 60% 股票/40% 债券。2025 年 AUM 约 500 亿美元；历史年回报率 7-8%，2025 年由于股票上涨回报率约 12%。

### 5.6.3 私募市场（私募股权和风险投资）Private Markets (Private Equity and Venture Capital)

**Operations:** Funds buy private companies (not public), optimize (e.g., management changes, synergies), exit via sale/IPO after 5-10 years. Structured as limited partnerships: GP (manager) handles investments; LPs (investors) commit capital. Fees: 1-3% management + 20% carried interest (profits after return hurdle). Illiquid; lockups 7-10 years, periodic distributions.
**运作**：基金购买私有公司（非上市），进行优化（如管理层变更、协同效应），5-10 年后通过出售/IPO 退出。结构为有限合伙制：GP（管理者）处理投资；LP（投资者）承诺资本。费用：1-3% 管理费 + 20% 附带权益（超过回报门槛后的利润）。流动性差；锁定期 7-10 年，定期分配。

| Example: Private equity：MooMoo App——理财——另类资产<br />示例：私募股权：MooMoo App——理财——另类资产 | ![](<images/入门考试学习资料 Learning Resources for Entry Knowledge Test-image5-1.png>) |
| -------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |

**Investment Points:**
**投资要点：**

* **Benefits:** High potential returns (10-15% historically), diversification from public markets; access to growth companies.
* **优势**：高潜在回报（历史 10-15%），与公开市场分散风险；接触成长型公司。

* **Risks:** Illiquidity, high fees, company failure (esp. VC: 80% startups fail); J-curve (early losses, later gains).
* **风险**：流动性差、高费用、公司倒闭（尤其是风投：80% 初创企业失败）；J 曲线（早期亏损，后期盈利）。

Suitability: Accredited investors (high net worth); long-term, aggressive goals. Minimums $1M+.
适用性：合格投资者（高净值）；长期、激进目标。最低门槛 100 万美元以上。

### 5.6.4 **收益型基金 Income fund**

The primary objective of an income fund is to provide current revenue (not growth) for investors. This type of fund invests most of its assets in a diversified portfolio of (a) debt securities that pay interest and in preferred and (b) common stock of companies that are known to pay consistent dividends in cash.&#x20;
收益型基金的主要目标是为投资者提供当前收入（而非增长）。此类基金将其大部分资产投资于多元化的组合，包括 (a) 支付利息的债务证券和优先股，以及 (b) 已知持续支付现金股息的公司的普通股。

### 5.6.5 **增长型基金Growth fund**

Growth funds invest most of their assets in a diversified portfolio of the common stock of relatively new and existing companies, looking for big increases in the stock prices. Growth funds offer a higher potential for growth but usually at a higher risk for the investor. This type of fund is ideal for an investor who’s looking for long-term capital appreciation potential.&#x20;
增长型基金将其大部分资产投资于相对较新和现有公司的普通股的多元化组合，寻求股价的大幅上涨。增长型基金提供更高的增长潜力，但通常对投资者来说风险也更高。此类基金非常适合寻求长期资本增值潜力的投资者。

Because of the inherent risk of investing in growth funds, they’re better for younger investors who can take the risk because they have more time to recover their losses.&#x20;
由于投资增长型基金的固有风险，它们更适合能够承担风险的年轻投资者，因为他们有更多时间来弥补损失。

Some growth funds are labeled as *aggressive growth funds* because the securities it invests in are even riskier than those of a standard growth fund.&#x20;
一些增长型基金被称为*激进增长型基金*，因为其投资的证券比标准增长型基金的风险更高。

### 5.6.6 **专业（行业）基金 Specialized (sector) fund**

A specialized or sector fund is a type of fund that invests primarily in the securities of a single industry. A specialized fund may invest only in financial services, healthcare, automotive stocks, technology securities, and so on. Because specialized funds are limited in their investments, you can assume that in many cases, they’re a little riskier (more volatile) than the average fund.&#x20;
专业或行业基金是一种主要投资于单一行业证券的基金。专业基金可能仅投资于金融服务、医疗保健、汽车股票、科技证券等。由于专业基金的投资受到限制，你可以假设在许多情况下，它们的风险（波动性）比普通基金稍高。

### 5.6.7 **国际或全球基金 International or global fund**

An international fund invests in companies based anywhere outside of the investor’s home country. A global fund invests in securities located anywhere in the world, including the investor’s home country. Although international and global funds may be good to round out a portfolio, they aren’t without their risks. Along with the risk that investors face by just investing in securities in general, holders of international and global funds also face currency risk, which is the risk that the currency exchange rate between the U.S. and foreign issuers will hurt investors. There’s also the additional risk that politics in a particular country will harm the value of the fund.
国际基金投资于投资者本国以外的任何地方的公司。全球基金投资于世界各地的证券，包括投资者的本国。虽然国际和全球基金可能有助于完善投资组合，但它们并非没有风险。除了投资者普遍面临的证券投资风险外，国际和全球基金的持有者还面临货币风险，即美国和外国发行人之间的汇率会损害投资者的利益。此外，还有一个额外的风险，即特定国家的政治会损害基金的价值。

### 5.6.8 **指数基金 Index fund**

This type of fund invests in securities that are similar to a particular stock or bond index. Rather than being actively managed, these types of funds are passively managed, which means investors can expect lower or no management fees. Investors can expect that the NAV of the fund will increase or decrease based on the movement of the benchmark index (S\&P 500, DJIA, and so on) the fund mimics.&#x20;
此类基金投资于与特定股票或债券指数相似的证券。这些类型的基金不是主动管理的，而是被动管理的，这意味着投资者可以预期较低或没有管理费。投资者可以预期基金的 NAV 将根据基金模仿的基准指数（标准普尔 500、道琼斯指数等）的变动而增加或减少。

### 5.6.9 **对冲基金 Hedge funds**

Hedge funds hold a pool of investments and are professionally managed like mutual (open-end) funds but have a lot more flexibility. Hedge funds are typically much more aggressive in nature and may buy securities on margin, sell securities short, purchase or sell options, and so on in an attempt to maximize gains.
对冲基金持有一组投资，并且像共同（开放式）基金一样由专业人士管理，但具有更大的灵活性。对冲基金本质上通常更加激进，可能会融资购买证券、卖空证券、买卖期权等，以试图实现收益最大化。

## 5.7 **了解主要基金费用 Understanding Key Fund Fees**

### 5.7.1 **前端收费（销售负担） Class A: Front-End Load (Sales Load)**

* **What it is:** A one-time sales charge paid when you buy shares in the fund. It's a percentage of your investment. The investor pays the load when purchasing shares of the fund. These funds typically are better for long-term investors because they usually have lower expense ratios, and they have breakpoints for large dollar purchases. For example, if you invest $10,000 in a fund with a 1% front-end load, $100 goes to the fee, and only $9,900 is invested.
* **定义**：购买基金份额时支付的一次性销售费用。它是你投资额的百分比。投资者在购买基金份额时支付佣金。这些基金通常更适合长期投资者，因为它们通常具有较低的费用率，并且对于大额购买有断点优惠。例如，如果你向收取 1% 前端费用的基金投资 10,000 美元，100 美元用于费用，只有 9,900 美元用于投资。

* **What it means:** This fee compensates brokers or salespeople for selling the fund. It reduces the amount of money that starts working for you right away.
* **含义**：这笔费用用于补偿经纪人或销售人员销售基金。它减少了立即为你工作的资金数额。

* **Applicable products:** Primarily mutual funds (load funds). Not common in money market funds, ETFs, or private markets. Many funds are now "no-load," meaning no front-end charge.
* **适用产品**：主要是共同基金（收费基金）。在货币市场基金、ETF 或私募市场中不常见。许多基金现在是“免佣”的，即没有前端费用。

### 5.7.2 **后端收费 （递延销售费用）Class B: Back-End Load (Deferred Sales Charge)**

* **What it is:** A sales charge paid when you sell or redeem shares. It often decreases over time. Also called contingent deferred sales charge. The investor pays the load when redeeming shares of the fund. These funds have higher expense ratios than Class A but often convert to Class A if held for many years. Very few mutual fund companies offer Class B shares at this point.&#x20;
* **定义**：出售或赎回份额时支付的销售费用。它通常随时间递减。也称为或有递延销售费用。投资者在赎回基金份额时支付佣金。这些基金的费用率高于 A 类，但如果持有多年，通常会转换为 A 类。目前很少有共同基金公司提供 B 类份额。

* **What it means:** Encourages long-term holding; the fee covers distribution costs if you exit early.
* **含义**：鼓励长期持有；如果你提前退出，费用将覆盖分销成本。

* **Applicable products:** Some mutual funds (load funds). Rare in other products like ETFs or private funds.
* **适用产品**：一些共同基金（收费基金）。在 ETF 或私募基金等其他产品中很少见。

### 5.7.3 **均衡佣金 Class C: level load**

The investors pay a periodic fee (usually, annually) over the time period that they hold the fund. These funds have higher expense ratios than Class A and have an exit fee, which is often eliminated after a year or two. These funds typically are the best option for short-term investors.&#x20;
投资者在持有基金期间支付定期费用（通常是每年）。这些基金的费用率高于 A 类，并且有退出费，通常在一两年后取消。这些基金通常是短期投资者的最佳选择。

### 5.7.4 **免佣基金 Class D: No-load**

Investors don’t pay a sales charge but may be charged some sort of transaction fee. Unlike load mutual funds, no-load funds may not charge a 12b-1 fee greater than 25 basis points.&#x20;
投资者不支付销售费用，但可能会被收取某种交易费。与收费共同基金不同，免佣基金收取的 12b-1 费用不得超过 25 个基点。

### 5.7.5 **管理费 Management Fee**

* **What it is**: An annual fee charged as a percentage of the fund's assets under management (AUM). For example, 1% on $100,000 means $1,000 per year.
* **定义**：按基金管理资产 (AUM) 的百分比收取的年费。例如，100,000 美元的 1% 意味着每年 1,000 美元。

* **What it means**: Pays the fund manager for selecting investments, research, and day-to-day operations. It's part of the overall expense ratio and deducted automatically from the fund's returns.
* **含义**：支付给基金经理用于选择投资、研究和日常运营。它是总费用率的一部分，并自动从基金回报中扣除。

* **Applicable products**: All funds, including mutual funds, money market funds, hybrid funds, ETFs, hedge funds, and private equity. In hedge funds, it's often 2%; in private equity, 1-3% of committed capital.
* **适用产品**：所有基金，包括共同基金、货币市场基金、混合基金、ETF、对冲基金和私募股权。在对冲基金中，通常为 2%；在私募股权中，为承诺资本的 1-3%。

### 5.7.6 **绩效费（激励费用或附带权益费） Performance Fee (Incentive Fee or Carried Interest)**

* **What it is**: A fee based on the fund's profits, typically 20% of gains above a certain threshold (hurdle rate, e.g., 8% return). Often includes a "high-water mark" – no fee until the fund recovers from losses.
* **定义**：基于基金利润的费用，通常为超过特定门槛（回报率门槛，如 8% 回报）收益的 20%。通常包括“高水位线”——在基金弥补损失之前不收费。

* **What it means**: Aligns the manager's incentives with investors; they earn more only if the fund performs well. In private equity, it's called "carried interest" and paid on profits from selling companies.
* **含义**：使管理者的激励与投资者保持一致；只有基金表现良好，他们才能赚更多。在私募股权中，它被称为“附带权益”，并从出售公司的利润中支付。

* **Applicable products**: Mainly alternative investments like hedge funds and private equity/venture capital funds. Not typical in mutual funds, money market funds, or hybrid funds, which focus on management fees.
* **适用产品**：主要是对冲基金和私募股权/风险投资基金等另类投资。在专注于管理费的共同基金、货币市场基金或混合基金中不典型。

| **8.What is a characteristic of hedge funds?**<br />**8. 对冲基金的一个特征是什么？**<br />A) More aggressive and flexible, using margin and options.<br />A) 更激进和灵活，使用保证金和期权。<br />B) Limited to traditional assets only.<br />B) 仅限于传统资产。<br />C) Low risk and stable NAV.<br />C) 低风险和稳定的 NAV。 | **Answer: A**<br />**答案：A**<br />**Explanation:** Hedge funds are aggressive, using strategies like margin, short selling, and options to maximize gains.<br />**解释：** 对冲基金是激进的，使用保证金、卖空和期权等策略来实现收益最大化。 |

# 6. 常见指数(**Common Index)**

> 考试小贴士：本章节题目占比为6-8%左右。
>
> * 6.1和6.2为纯概念考察，你需要知道两种收益计算方法的区别和逻辑以及三种指数构建方式的相关概念和区别。
>
> * 6.3部分需要对常见的大类资产指数有初步的印象和认知，考试内容仅限制于文中提到的主要指数。6.4部分只需要了解常见的指数投资策略，考试内容仅停留在概念。
>
> * 产品层面：知道常见资产代表性ETF的信息和代码。

## 6.1 **如何计算指数收益（两种关键类型）How to Calculate Index Returns (Two Key Types)**

Indexes track the "average performance" of a group of stocks (e.g., S\&P 500 tracks 500 large U.S. companies). Returns measure how much the index has gone up/down.
指数追踪一组股票的“平均表现”（例如，标准普尔 500 指数追踪 500 家大型美国公司）。回报率衡量指数上涨/下跌了多少。

* **Price Return**: Price return only looks at changes in the index level itself, ignoring any dividends paid by the companies in the index. It shows pure price appreciation (or depreciation).&#x20;
* **价格回报**：价格回报仅关注指数水平本身的变化，忽略指数中公司支付的任何股息。它显示纯粹的价格增值（或贬值）。

  * Price Return = \[(Ending Index Level - Beginning Index Level) / Beginning Index Level] × 100%
  * 价格回报 = \[(期末指数水平 - 期初指数水平) / 期初指数水平] × 100%

  * Example: For the S\&P 500 in 2025, it started at approximately 5,903 open) and ended at 6,845. Price Return = \[(6,845 - 5,903) / 5,903] × 100% = 15.96%. This means the index's price rose by about 16% due to stock price increases alone.
  * 示例：对于 2025 年的标准普尔 500 指数，其开盘价约为 5,903，收盘价为 6,845。价格回报 = \[(6,845 - 5,903) / 5,903] × 100% = 15.96%。这意味着仅由于股价上涨，指数价格上涨了约 16%。

* **Total Return**: Total return includes both price changes and dividends paid (assuming they're reinvested back into the index). It gives a fuller picture of what an investor might actually earn.
* **总回报**：总回报包括价格变化和支付的股息（假设它们被再投资回指数）。它更全面地反映了投资者实际可能获得的收益。

  * Total Return = \[(Ending Index Value with Reinvested Dividends - Beginning Index Value) / Beginning Index Value] × 100%
  * 总回报 = \[(含再投资股息的期末指数价值 - 期初指数价值) / 期初指数价值] × 100%

  * Example: Using the same S\&P 500 for 2025, the total return (with dividends reinvested) was 17.9%. This is higher than the price return because dividends from S\&P 500 companies added extra gains when reinvested.&#x20;
  * 示例：使用相同的 2025 年标准普尔 500 指数，总回报（含再投资股息）为 17.9%。这高于价格回报，因为标准普尔 500 指数公司的股息在再投资时增加了额外收益。

## 6.2 **指数加权方法（关键要点：指数如何对股票进行“排序”）Index Weighting Methods (Key Focus: How Indexes "Rank" Stocks)**

Weighting = how much each stock affects the index’s performance. Think of it as a "voting system"—higher-weighted stocks have more say in the index’s return.
加权 = 每只股票对指数表现的影响程度。把它想象成一个“投票系统”——权重较高的股票在指数回报中有更大的发言权。

| **Method**<br>**方法** | **Explanation**<br>**解释** | **Example**<br>**示例** |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Price-Weighted**<br>**价格加权** | Stocks with higher per-share prices have more weight. A $200 stock affects the index 10x more than a $20 stock.<br>每股价格较高的股票权重较大。200 美元的股票对指数的影响是 20 美元股票的 10 倍。 | Dow Jones Industrial Average (DJIA: 30 large U.S. stocks). Today Apple’s price is around $267, Coca-Cola’s is around $68—Apple’s price moves have a much bigger impact on DJIA than Coca-Cola’s.<br>道琼斯工业平均指数 (DJIA: 30 家大型美国公司)。今天苹果的价格约为 267 美元，可口可乐约为 68 美元——苹果的价格变动对 DJIA 的影响远大于可口可乐。 |
| **Market-Cap Weighted**<br>**市值加权** | Stocks with bigger "total value" (shares outstanding × price) have more weight. A $2T company affects the index 10x more than a $200B company.<br>“总价值”（流通股 × 价格）较大的股票权重较大。2 万亿美元的公司对指数的影响是 2000 亿美元公司的 10 倍。 | S\&P 500 (most important U.S. index).<br>标准普尔 500 指数（最重要的美国指数）。 |
| **Equal-Weighted**<br>**等权重** | Every stock has the same weight, no matter its price or size. A small $10B company affects the index as much as a $2T company.<br>每只股票的权重相同，无论其价格或规模如何。一家 100 亿美元的小公司对指数的影响与一家 2 万亿美元的公司相同。 | S\&P 500 Equal Weight Index.<br>标准普尔 500 等权重指数。 |

## 6.4 **常用指数投资策略 Common Index Investing Strategies**

### 6.4.1 **买入并持有 Buy and Hold**

Invest in a broad index fund and keep it long-term, ignoring short-term fluctuations.&#x20;
投资于广泛的指数基金并长期持有，忽略短期波动。

**Purpose:** Compound returns over time via market growth. Market Environment: Long-term bull markets.
**目的**：通过市场增长实现长期复利回报。市场环境：长期牛市。

**Example:** $10,000 in SPY in 2016 grew to around $35,000 by 2026 (13% annualized), thanks to compounding.
**示例**：2016 年向 SPY 投资 10,000 美元，到 2026 年增长至约 35,000 美元（年化 13%），这要归功于复利。

### 6.4.2 **美元成本平均（DCA）Dollar-Cost Averaging (DCA)**

Invest fixed amounts regularly (e.g., monthly), buying more shares when prices are low.&#x20;
定期（例如每月）投资固定金额，价格低时购买更多份额。

**Purpose:** Reduce timing risk and average costs. Market Environment: Volatile or uncertain markets.
**目的**：降低择时风险和平均成本。市场环境：波动或不确定的市场。

**Example:** Invest $500 monthly in SPY; but in dips like Covid crash and 2025.04, you buy more cheaper shares, try to boosting overall returns.
**示例**：每月向 SPY 投资 500 美元；但在新冠崩盘和 2025.04 等低谷期，你买入更多便宜的份额，试图提高整体回报。

### 6.4.3 **核心 - 卫星策略 Core-Satellite**

Use a core index fund for stability, add "satellites" like sector ETFs for growth.&#x20;
使用核心指数基金保持稳定性，添加行业 ETF 等“卫星”以实现增长。

**Purpose:** Balance safety with potential outperformance. Market Environment: Growth phases.
**目的**：平衡安全性与潜在的跑赢大盘。市场环境：增长阶段。

**Example:** 70% in SPY ($7,000 at $585), 30% in XLK tech ETF; in 2025, this beat plain SPY by 2%
**示例**：70% 投资于 SPY（7,000 美元，价格 585 美元），30% 投资于 XLK 科技 ETF；在 2025 年，这比单纯持有 SPY 高出 2%。

### 6.4.4 **再平衡 Rebalancing**

Periodically adjust allocations to maintain targets (e.g., 60% stocks via index funds).&#x20;
定期调整配置以维持目标（例如通过指数基金持有 60% 股票）。

**Purpose:** Control risk and lock gains.
**目的**：控制风险并锁定收益。

**Example:** If SPY surges, sell some to buy bonds, keeping portfolio balanced.
**示例**：如果 SPY 飙升，卖出一些买入债券，保持投资组合平衡。

# 7. 财报分析 **Financial Statement Analysis**

> 考试小贴士：本章节题目占比为15%左右。
>
> * 7.1为重点考察内容，你需要了解财报的目的、意义以及常见的两种会计准则。
>
> * 7.2为重点考察内容，你需要了解利润表的是什么、有什么用以及利润表中的重要成分。
>
> * 7.3-7.4涉及资产负债表和现金流量表，在本次考试中重要性不及7.1的利润表，大家知道概念即可。
>
> * 7.5中常见的财务指标为重点考察内容，需要掌握相关比率的作用以及知晓如何计算。
>
> * 产品层面：知道如何在MooMoo App中查找对应的财务报表、App中常见的财务指标如何理解和计算。

## 7.1 **财务报表的目的/意义以及通用会计准则 The Purpose/significance of Financial Statements, and Common Accounting Standard**

Financial statements are formal records that summarize a company's financial activities over a specific period. They provide a structured overview of a firm's assets, liabilities, revenues, expenses, and cash flows, enabling stakeholders to assess its overall health and performance.
财务报表是总结公司在特定时期内财务活动的正式记录。它们提供了公司资产、负债、收入、费用和现金流量的结构化概览，使利益相关者能够评估其整体健康状况和业绩。

In the US stock market, public companies are required by the Securities and Exchange Commission (SEC) to file these statements regularly—typically quarterly (via Form 10-Q) and annually (via Form 10-K)—to promote transparency and protect investors. This regulatory mandate ensures that market participants have access to reliable data, reducing information asymmetry and supporting fair trading on exchanges like the NYSE or Nasdaq.
在美国股票市场，上市公司必须按照美国证券交易委员会 (SEC) 的要求定期提交这些报表——通常是每季度（通过 10-Q 表格）和每年（通过 10-K 表格）——以促进透明度和保护投资者。这一监管强制要求确保市场参与者能够获得可靠的数据，减少信息不对称，并支持纽交所或纳斯达克等交易所的公平交易。

The core purpose is threefold:
核心目的有三个：

* **Transparency for Investors**: Statements allow shareholders to evaluate how their capital is being used. For instance, they reveal profitability trends, debt levels, and operational efficiency, which inform decisions on buying, holding, or selling stocks.
* **对投资者的透明度**：报表允许股东评估其资本的使用情况。例如，它们揭示了盈利趋势、债务水平和运营效率，为买入、持有或卖出股票的决策提供信息。

* **Accountability for Management**: They hold executives responsible by documenting results against goals, often influencing corporate governance, such as board votes or executive compensation.
* **管理层的问责制**：它们通过记录结果与目标的对比来让高管承担责任，通常会影响公司治理，如董事会投票或高管薪酬。

* **Market Efficiency**: By standardizing reporting, statements facilitate comparisons across companies, helping the market price stocks accurately based on fundamentals rather than speculation.
* **市场效率**：通过标准化报告，报表促进了公司之间的比较，帮助市场根据基本面而非投机来准确地为股票定价。

| ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |

### 7.1.1 **财务报表分析的意义 The Significance of Financial Statement Analysis**

Analyzing financial statements involves reviewing and interpreting the data to draw conclusions about a company's viability, growth potential, and risks. This process is critical in financial markets because it translates raw numbers into actionable insights, directly influencing stock prices and investment strategies.
分析财务报表涉及审查和解释数据，以得出关于公司生存能力、增长潜力和风险的结论。这个过程在金融市场中至关重要，因为它将原始数字转化为可操作的见解，直接影响股价和投资策略。

Key benefits include:
主要好处包括：

* **Informed Investment Decisions**: Analysis helps identify undervalued or overvalued stocks by examining metrics like earnings growth or debt ratios. It reveals whether a company can generate returns exceeding its cost of capital, a key factor in stock performance.
* **明智的投资决策**：分析通过检查盈利增长或债务比率等指标，帮助识别被低估或高估的股票。它揭示了公司是否能产生超过其资本成本的回报，这是股票表现的关键因素。

* **Risk Assessment**: It uncovers vulnerabilities, such as high leverage or declining cash flows, allowing investors to avoid potential losses during market downturns.
* **风险评估**：它揭示了脆弱性，如高杠杆或现金流下降，使投资者能够在市场低迷期间避免潜在损失。

* **Performance Forecasting**: By spotting trends, analysts can predict future earnings, which drives buy/sell recommendations and impacts market sentiment.
* **业绩预测**：通过发现趋势，分析师可以预测未来收益，这推动了买入/卖出建议并影响市场情绪。

### 7.1.2 **通用会计准则 Common Accounting Standards**

Financial statements are prepared under standardized rules to ensure consistency. The two most prevalent frameworks are US Generally Accepted Accounting Principles (GAAP) and International Financial Reporting Standards (IFRS). While GAAP is mandatory for US public companies, IFRS is used by many international firms, including those with US listings via American Depositary Receipts (ADRs).
财务报表是根据标准化规则编制的，以确保一致性。两种最普遍的框架是美国通用会计准则 (US GAAP) 和国际财务报告准则 (IFRS)。虽然 GAAP 对美国上市公司是强制性的，但许多国际公司使用 IFRS，包括那些通过美国存托凭证 (ADR) 在美国上市的公司。

* **US GAAP**: Developed by the Financial Accounting Standards Board (FASB), GAAP is rules-based, providing detailed guidelines for specific scenarios. It emphasizes precision and is tailored to the US legal environment, making it ideal for complex transactions in litigious markets.
* **US GAAP**：由财务会计准则委员会 (FASB) 制定，GAAP 是基于规则的，为特定场景提供详细指南。它强调精确性，并针对美国法律环境量身定制，使其非常适合诉讼市场中的复杂交易。

* **IFRS**: Issued by the International Accounting Standards Board (IASB), IFRS is principles-based, offering broader guidance that requires professional judgment. It's adopted in over 140 countries, promoting global comparability but allowing more flexibility.
* **IFRS**：由国际会计准则理事会 (IASB) 发布，IFRS 是基于原则的，提供需要专业判断的更广泛指导。它被 140 多个国家采用，促进了全球可比性，但也允许更大的灵活性。

* **Applicable Regions**
* **适用地区**

US GAAP is predominantly applied in the United States, where it is mandated by the Securities and Exchange Commission (SEC) for all domestic public companies filing financial reports. This ensures uniformity in the US capital markets, facilitating investor comparisons and regulatory oversight. While some foreign entities may voluntarily adopt US GAAP for US listings or to align with American investors, it is not widely used outside the US. For instance, in 2026, the US remains the primary jurisdiction requiring GAAP, with no significant international adoptions beyond specific cross-border scenarios.
US GAAP 主要适用于美国，美国证券交易委员会 (SEC) 强制要求所有提交财务报告的国内上市公司使用该准则。这确保了美国资本市场的统一性，便于投资者比较和监管监督。虽然一些外国实体可能会为了在美国上市或与美国投资者接轨而自愿采用 US GAAP，但它在美国以外并未广泛使用。例如，在 2026 年，美国仍然是要求 GAAP 的主要司法管辖区，除特定的跨境情况外，国际上没有显著采用。

In contrast, IFRS is the dominant standard internationally, required for public companies in over 140 jurisdictions worldwide as of 2026. This includes all European Union member states (e.g., Germany, France), the United Kingdom, Australia, New Zealand, Canada (for publicly accountable enterprises), South Africa, Brazil, and South Korea. In Japan, IFRS is permitted for certain domestic companies and required for some consolidated financial statements, while countries like Mexico and Singapore have fully adopted it for listed entities. The widespread use of IFRS promotes cross-border investment by standardizing reporting, though it is not mandatory for US domestic firms—foreign private issuers listed on US exchanges can use IFRS without reconciliation to GAAP.
相比之下，IFRS 是国际上的主导标准，截至 2026 年，全球 140 多个司法管辖区的上市公司都必须采用该标准。这包括所有欧盟成员国（如德国、法国）、英国、澳大利亚、新西兰、加拿大（针对负有公共责任的企业）、南非、巴西和韩国。在日本，某些国内公司被允许使用 IFRS，并且某些合并财务报表也要求使用 IFRS，而墨西哥和新加坡等国家已完全对上市实体采用该标准。IFRS 的广泛使用通过标准化报告促进了跨境投资，尽管它对美国国内公司不是强制性的——在美国交易所上市的外国私人发行人可以使用 IFRS 而无需调节至 GAAP。

## 7.2 **损益表分析 Analyzing the Income Statement**

### 7.2.1 什么是**损益**表？它为何重要？What Is the Income Statement and Why Does It Matter?

The income statement, also known as the statement of operations, profit and loss statement, or P\&L, provides a summary of a company's revenues, expenses, gains, and losses over a defined period, such as a fiscal quarter or year. It calculates net income (or loss) by subtracting expenses from revenues, which represents the profit available to shareholders after all costs. This statement is accrual-based, meaning it records transactions when they occur economically, not necessarily when cash changes hands, allowing for a more accurate picture of performance.
损益表，也称为经营报表、损益表或 P\&L，总结了公司在特定时期（如财政季度或年度）内的收入、费用、利得和损失。它通过从收入中减去费用来计算净利润（或亏损），这代表了扣除所有成本后股东可获得的利润。该报表基于权责发生制，意味着它在交易经济上发生时记录，而不一定是在现金转手时，从而能够更准确地反映业绩。

In the US financial markets, the income statement is a core component of SEC filings like Form 10-Q (quarterly) and 10-K (annual). It directly influences stock valuations through metrics like earnings per share (EPS), which analysts use to compute price-to-earnings (P/E) ratios. For analysts, it's essential for assessing a company's profitability trends, operational efficiency, and growth potential. Strong earnings can fuel stock rallies, while misses often lead to sell-offs, impacting trading volumes and client portfolios.
在美国金融市场，损益表是 SEC 文件（如 10-Q 季度报表和 10-K 年度报表）的核心组成部分。它通过每股收益 (EPS) 等指标直接影响股票估值，分析师使用 EPS 来计算市盈率 (P/E)。对于分析师而言，评估公司的盈利趋势、运营效率和增长潜力至关重要。强劲的收益可以推动股价上涨，而未达预期往往导致抛售，影响交易量和客户投资组合。

Key significance:
主要意义：

* **Profitability Assessment**: Reveals how effectively a company converts revenues into profits, highlighting strengths (e.g., high margins) or weaknesses (e.g., rising costs).
* **盈利能力评估**：揭示公司将收入转化为利润的效率，突出优势（如高利润率）或劣势（如成本上升）。

* **Trend Analysis**: Year-over-year (YoY) or quarter-over-quarter (QoQ) comparisons show growth trajectories, such as expanding revenues or improving margins, which inform buy/sell recommendations.
* **趋势分析**：同比 (YoY) 或环比 (QoQ) 比较显示增长轨迹，如收入扩大或利润率提高，为买入/卖出建议提供信息。

* **Forecasting and Valuation**: Serves as the foundation for earnings models; analysts project future EPS to derive target prices using discounted cash flow (DCF) or multiples.
* **预测和估值**：作为收益模型的基础；分析师预测未来 EPS，利用贴现现金流 (DCF) 或倍数法得出目标价格。

* **Market Impact**: Earnings releases based on this statement drive volatility—e.g., the NYSE and Nasdaq see average daily volume spikes of 20-30% during earnings seasons as investors react.
* **市场影响**：基于此报表的收益发布推动波动性——例如，在财报季，随着投资者做出反应，纽交所和纳斯达克的日均交易量会激增 20-30%。

* **Risk Identification**: Flags issues like one-time charges or margin erosion, helping brokerages advise on hedging or diversification.
* **风险识别**：标记一次性费用或利润率侵蚀等问题，帮助券商建议对冲或多元化。

* **Regulatory Compliance**: Ensures transparency under SEC rules, preventing fraud like revenue inflation, which could lead to restatements and stock plunges.
* **监管合规**：确保符合 SEC 规则的透明度，防止收入虚增等欺诈行为，这可能导致重述和股价暴跌。

* **Overview of Apple's Income Statement: go through the whole statement to see the components of an income statement, and we will break it down in this section.**
* **苹果损益表概览：浏览整个报表以查看损益表的组成部分，我们将在本节中对其进行分解。**

### 7.2.2 损益表的主要组成部分 Key Components of the Income Statement

Public companies typically present a multi-step income statement under US GAAP, which separates operating from non-operating activities for clearer analysis. Below, we detail each major line item, its definition, recognition principles (integrated where relevant), significance, and applications, with expanded explanations including GAAP vs. IFRS differences and real examples.
上市公司通常根据 US GAAP 呈现多步损益表，将经营活动与非经营活动分开，以便更清晰地分析。下面，我们将详细介绍每个主要项目、其定义、确认原则（相关处已整合）、意义和应用，并附有扩展解释，包括 GAAP 与 IFRS 的差异和真实案例。

1. **Revenue / Net Sales** This is the top line, representing income from the company's primary business activities, such as selling products or providing services. "Net" sales deduct returns, allowances (e.g., for defective goods), and discounts from gross sales. Revenue may be segmented by product lines, geography, or business units in the notes.
1. **收入 / 净销售额** 这是第一行，代表来自公司主要业务活动的收入，如销售产品或提供服务。“净”销售额从总销售额中扣除退货、折让（例如缺陷商品）和折扣。收入可能会在附注中按产品线、地理区域或业务部门进行细分。

**Example – Microsoft Corporation (MSFT)**: In FY2025 (ended June 30, 2025), Microsoft reported total revenue of $281.724 billion, including cloud services (Azure), revenue from subscriptions like Microsoft 365 and personal computing
**案例 – 微软公司 (MSFT)**：在 2025 财年（截至 2025 年 6 月 30 日），微软报告总收入为 2817.24 亿美元，包括云服务 (Azure)、Microsoft 365 等订阅收入和个人计算业务。

| How to find detailed revenue breakdowns in MooMoo App: Select a company——公司——财务——主营构成:<br />如何在 MooMoo App 中查找详细的收入明细：选择公司——公司——财务——主营构成： | ![](<images/入门考试学习资料 Learning Resources for Entry Knowledge Test-image-82.png>) |
| -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |

* **Cost of Goods Sold (COGS) / Cost of Sales / Cost of Revenue** Direct costs attributable to producing or delivering the goods/services sold, including raw materials, direct labor, manufacturing overhead, freight-in, and inventory-related expenses. For service firms, it includes costs like server maintenance or content acquisition.
* **销货成本 (COGS) / 销售成本 / 收入成本** 归因于生产或交付所售商品/服务的直接成本，包括原材料、直接人工、制造费用、进货运费和库存相关费用。对于服务公司，它包括服务器维护或内容获取等成本。

**Example – Amazon.com Inc. (AMZN)**: In FY2024, Amazon's cost of sales was $326.288 billion against net sales of $637.959 billion, yielding a gross profit of $311.671 billion and gross margin of 48.8%. This includes fulfillment, shipping, and AWS infrastructure costs, recognized as revenue is earned. The margin improvement (from prior years) reflects efficiency gains in logistics.
**案例 – 亚马逊公司 (AMZN)**：在 2024 财年，亚马逊的销售成本为 3262.88 亿美元，净销售额为 6379.59 亿美元，产生毛利 3116.71 亿美元，毛利率为 48.8%。这包括履约、运输和 AWS 基础设施成本，在赚取收入时确认。利润率的提高（与往年相比）反映了物流效率的提升。

| How to find COGS in MooMoo App: Select a company——公司——利润表——营业总成本：<br />如何在 MooMoo App 中查找 COGS：选择公司——公司——利润表——营业总成本： | ![](<images/入门考试学习资料 Learning Resources for Entry Knowledge Test-image-83.png>) |
| ----------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |

* **Gross Profit**: Revenue minus COGS. This subtotal isolates profitability from core operations before indirect costs.
* **毛利**：收入减去 COGS。此小计在扣除间接成本之前隔离了核心业务的盈利能力。

| How to find gross profit in MooMoo App: Select a company——公司——利润表——毛利：<br />如何在 MooMoo App 中查找毛利：选择公司——公司——利润表——毛利： | ![](<images/入门考试学习资料 Learning Resources for Entry Knowledge Test-image-95.png>) |
| ---------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |

* **Operating Expenses (OpEx)** Indirect costs not directly tied to production, often broken into categories like:
* **营业费用 (OpEx)** 与生产不直接相关的间接成本，通常分为以下几类：

a. Selling, General & Administrative (SG\&A)**:** Includes marketing, salaries, rent, utilities, legal fees, and executive compensation.
a. 销售、一般及行政费用 (SG\&A)**:** 包括营销、工资、租金、水电费、法律费用和高管薪酬。

b. Research & Development (R\&D)**:** Costs for innovation, like lab expenses or clinical trials.
b. 研发费用 (R\&D)**:** 创新成本，如实验室费用或临床试验。

c. Depreciation & Amortization**:** Non-cash allocation of long-term asset costs (e.g., buildings, patents).
c. 折旧与摊销**:** 长期资产成本（如建筑物、专利）的非现金分摊。

d. Other**:** Stock-based compensation, restructuring charges.
d. 其他**:** 基于股票的薪酬、重组费用。

**Example – Pfizer Inc. (PFE)**: Pfizer's FY2024 report included R\&D expenses of $11.0 billion, expensed immediately under US GAAP, reflecting heavy investment in oncology and vaccines. Investment banks like Barclays adjust for capitalized IFRS peers when valuing, noting PFE's conservative approach avoids asset overstatements but pressures short-term EPS.
**案例 – 辉瑞公司 (PFE)**：辉瑞 2024 财年报告包括 110 亿美元的研发费用，根据 US GAAP 立即费用化，反映了在肿瘤学和疫苗方面的巨额投资。像巴克莱这样的投资银行在估值时会针对资本化的 IFRS 同行进行调整，指出辉瑞的保守方法避免了资产高估，但对短期 EPS 造成了压力。

| How to find operating expenses in MooMoo App: Select a company——公司——利润表——营业费用：<br />如何在 MooMoo App 中查找营业费用：选择公司——公司——利润表——营业费用： | ![](<images/入门考试学习资料 Learning Resources for Entry Knowledge Test-image-90.png>) |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |

* **Operating Income (EBIT – Earnings Before Interest & Taxes)** Gross profit minus operating expenses. Excludes financing and taxes for a pure view of operational performance.
* **营业利润 (EBIT – 息税前利润)** 毛利减去营业费用。排除融资和税收，以纯粹地反映运营绩效。

| How to find operating expenses in MooMoo App: Select a company——公司——利润表——营业利润：<br />如何在 MooMoo App 中查找营业利润：选择公司——公司——利润表——营业利润： | ![](<images/入门考试学习资料 Learning Resources for Entry Knowledge Test-image-81.png>) |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |

* **Non-Operating Income/Expense** Items outside core operations, like interest expense (from debt), interest income (from investments), gains/losses on asset sales, foreign exchange, or equity investments.
* **营业外收入/费用** 核心业务以外的项目，如利息费用（来自债务）、利息收入（来自投资）、资产出售损益、外汇或股权投资。

| How to find non-operating income/expenses in MooMoo App: Select a company——公司——利润表——营业外利息收入/费用；其他净收入/费用：<br />如何在 MooMoo App 中查找营业外收入/费用：选择公司——公司——利润表——营业外利息收入/费用；其他净收入/费用： | ![](<images/入门考试学习资料 Learning Resources for Entry Knowledge Test-image-107.png>) |
| -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |

* **Income Before Taxes (Pre-tax Income)** Operating income plus/minus non-operating items.
* **税前利润** 营业利润加上/减去营业外项目。

**Importance:** Shows earnings before tax effects, useful for international comparisons with varying rates.
**重要性：** 显示税前收益，有助于比较税率不同的国际公司。

* **Net Income (Net Earnings / Bottom Line)** Pre-tax income minus taxes, adjusted for discontinued operations or extraordinary items (rare under current standards).
* **净利润 (净收益 / 底线)** 税前利润减去税款，针对已终止经营业务或非常规项目（在现行标准下很少见）进行调整。

**Importance**: Profit attributable to shareholders; flows to retained earnings and supports dividends/buybacks.
**重要性**：归属于股东的利润；流入留存收益并支持股息/回购。

## 7.3 分析资产负债表 Analyzing the Balance Sheet

### 7.3.1 什么是资产负债表及其重要性 What Is the Balance Sheet and Why Does It Matter?

The balance sheet, also known as the statement of financial position, provides a snapshot of a company's financial position at a specific point in time, typically the end of a fiscal quarter (Form 10-Q) or year (Form 10-K). It lists what the company owns (assets), what it owes (liabilities), and the residual interest of shareholders (equity), adhering to the fundamental accounting equation: **Assets = Liabilities + Equity**. Unlike the income statement, which covers a period, the balance sheet is point-in-time and accrual-based, reflecting historical costs adjusted for depreciation, impairments, and fair value where applicable.
资产负债表，也称为财务状况表，提供了公司在特定时间点（通常是财政季度末（10-Q 表格）或年末（10-K 表格））的财务状况快照。它列出了公司拥有的（资产）、欠下的（负债）以及股东的剩余权益（权益），遵循基本会计等式：**资产 = 负债 + 权益**。与涵盖一段时间的损益表不同，资产负债表是时间点上的，基于权责发生制，反映了经折旧、减值和公允价值（如适用）调整后的历史成本。

In the US financial markets, the balance sheet is crucial for SEC filings, offering insights into a company's solvency, liquidity, and capital structure. It helps assess whether a firm can meet obligations, fund growth, or weather downturns, directly impacting stock valuations through ratios like debt-to-equity or return on assets (ROA). For analyst, it's vital for credit analysis (e.g., bond recommendations), M\&A due diligence, and risk management, as imbalances can signal distress or opportunities.
在美国金融市场，资产负债表对 SEC 备案至关重要，它提供了对公司偿债能力、流动性和资本结构的见解。它有助于评估公司是否能履行义务、为增长提供资金或抵御低迷，通过债务权益比或资产回报率 (ROA) 等比率直接影响股票估值。对于分析师来说，它对于信用分析（如债券建议）、并购尽职调查和风险管理至关重要，因为失衡可能预示着困境或机会。

Key significance:
主要意义：

* **Solvency and Stability Evaluation**: Shows leverage and asset quality, revealing if a company is over-indebted or asset-rich, which affects credit ratings and borrowing costs.
* **偿债能力和稳定性评估**：显示杠杆和资产质量，揭示公司是否负债过多或资产丰富，从而影响信用评级和借贷成本。

* **Liquidity Assessment**: Current assets vs. liabilities indicate short-term health, critical during market stresses like recessions.
* **流动性评估**：流动资产与负债的对比表明短期健康状况，这在经济衰退等市场压力期间至关重要。

* **Investment and Valuation Insights**: Equity components track shareholder value creation; asset breakdowns highlight intangibles or investments driving multiples like price-to-book (P/B).
* **投资和估值见解**：权益部分追踪股东价值创造；资产细分突出了推动市净率 (P/B) 等倍数的无形资产或投资。

* **Trend and Comparative Analysis**: Period-over-period changes spot growth (e.g., asset expansion) or red flags (e.g., rising debt); common-size analysis normalizes for peer comparisons.
* **趋势和比较分析**：通过各期变化发现增长（如资产扩张）或危险信号（如债务上升）；共同比分析使同行比较标准化。

* **Regulatory and Compliance Role**: Ensures adherence to GAAP/IFRS, preventing manipulations like off-balance-sheet financing, which could lead to SEC probes and price drops.
* **监管和合规作用**：确保遵守 GAAP/IFRS，防止资产负债表外融资等操纵行为，这可能导致 SEC 调查和价格下跌。

### 7.3.2 资产负债表的关键组成部分Key Components of the Balance Sheet

Public companies present classified balance sheets under US GAAP, separating current (within one year) from non-current items for better liquidity analysis. Below, we detail each major category and subcategory, integrating recognition principles (e.g., historical cost, fair value, impairment), significance, applications, and expanded explanations with GAAP vs. IFRS differences where relevant.
上市公司根据 US GAAP 呈现分类资产负债表，将流动项目（一年内）与非流动项目分开，以便更好地进行流动性分析。下面，我们详细介绍每个主要类别和子类别，整合确认原则（如历史成本、公允价值、减值）、意义、应用，并附有扩展解释，包括 GAAP 与 IFRS 的差异（如果相关）。

**Overview of Apple's balance sheet: go through the whole statement to see the components of a balance**
**苹果资产负债表概览：浏览整个报表以查看资产负债表的组成部分**

![](<images/入门考试学习资料 Learning Resources for Entry Knowledge Test-image-102.png>)

1. **Assets：**
1. **资产：**

**Assets are resources controlled by the company expected to provide future economic benefits.** They are recognized when probable benefits flow to the entity and costs/reliable measurements exist. Measured at historical cost less depreciation/amortization/impairments, or fair value for certain financial assets. IFRS allows revaluation models for PPE/intangibles, potentially inflating values vs. GAAP's cost model.
**资产是公司控制的预期提供未来经济利益的资源。** 当可能的利益流入实体且存在成本/可靠计量时确认。按历史成本减去折旧/摊销/减值计量，某些金融资产按公允价值计量。IFRS 允许 PPE/无形资产的重估模型，这可能导致价值相对于 GAAP 的成本模型膨胀。

**Role in financial report**: Represent value-generating capacity; composition (e.g., cash-heavy vs. intangible-heavy) indicates business model and risk profile.
**在财务报告中的作用**：代表价值创造能力；构成（如现金密集型与无形资产密集型）表明商业模式和风险状况。

* **Tangible Assets**
* **有形资产**

Tangible assets are physical, touchable resources owned by a company with economic value, such as land, buildings, machinery, vehicles, or equipment, typically reported as property, plant, and equipment (PPE) net of depreciation in the non-current assets section of the balance sheet.
有形资产是公司拥有的具有经济价值的实物、可触摸资源，如土地、建筑物、机械、车辆或设备，通常在资产负债表的非流动资产部分报告为扣除折旧后的物业、厂房和设备 (PPE)。

* **Intangible Assets**
* **无形资产**

Intangible assets are non-physical assets with economic value, such as patents, trademarks, copyrights, software, customer relationships, or brand names, reported as non-current assets on the balance sheet.
无形资产是具有经济价值的非实物资产，如专利、商标、版权、软件、客户关系或品牌名称，在资产负债表上报告为非流动资产。

* **Liabilities**
* **负债**

Obligations to transfer assets or provide services. Recognized when probable outflow and measurable. Measure leverage, high levels increase bankruptcy risk but amplify returns. Debt ratios are derived from liabilities and assess default risk.
转移资产或提供服务的义务。当可能流出且可计量时确认。衡量杠杆，高水平增加破产风险但放大回报。债务比率源自负债并评估违约风险。

* **Current Liabilities**
* **流动负债**

Current liabilities are short-term obligations on the balance sheet expected to be settled within one year or the operating cycle, such as accounts payable, short-term debt, accrued expenses, or deferred revenue, representing immediate claims on the company's assets.
流动负债是资产负债表上预计在一年或一个运营周期内结算的短期义务，如应付账款、短期债务、应计费用或递延收入，代表对公司资产的即时索赔。

| How to find current liabilities in MooMoo App: ——公司——资产负债表——流动负债:<br />如何在 MooMoo App 中查找流动负债：——公司——资产负债表——流动负债： | ![](<images/入门考试学习资料 Learning Resources for Entry Knowledge Test-image-108.png>) |
| ----------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |

* **Non-Current Liabilities**
* **非流动负债**

Non-current liabilities, also known as long-term liabilities, are obligations on the balance sheet not due for settlement within one year or the operating cycle, such as long-term debt, deferred tax liabilities, pension obligations, or leases.
非流动负债，也称为长期负债，是资产负债表上不在一年或一个运营周期内结算的义务，如长期债务、递延所得税负债、养老金义务或租赁。

| How to find non-current liabilities in MooMoo App: ——公司——资产负债表——非流动负债:<br />如何在 MooMoo App 中查找非流动负债：——公司——资产负债表——非流动负债： | ![](<images/入门考试学习资料 Learning Resources for Entry Knowledge Test-image-109.png>) |
| ---------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |

* **Equity**
* **权益**

Residual after liabilities; includes contributed capital and earned surplus. It is the Shareholder claims; growth via retained earnings signals strength.
负债后的剩余部分；包括投入资本和赚取的盈余。这是股东的索赔权；通过留存收益增长标志着实力。

* **Common Stock and Additional Paid-In Capital (APIC)**:&#x20;
* **普通股和资本公积 (APIC)**：

Common Stock represents the par or nominal value of shares issued to shareholders, while Additional Paid-In Capital (APIC), also known as share premium, captures the excess amount paid by investors above the par value during stock issuances. Together, they form part of shareholders' equity on the balance sheet, reflecting the initial capital contributed by owners through equity financing.
普通股代表发行给股东的股票的面值或名义价值，而资本公积 (APIC)，也称为股本溢价，捕获了投资者在股票发行期间支付的高于面值的超额金额。它们共同构成了资产负债表上股东权益的一部分，反映了所有者通过股权融资贡献的初始资本。

* **Retained Earnings / Accumulated Deficit**:&#x20;
* **留存收益 / 累计亏损**：

Retained Earnings represent the cumulative net profits a company has earned since inception, minus any dividends paid to shareholders or losses incurred. It's a key component of shareholders' equity on the balance sheet, showing the portion of earnings reinvested in the business rather than distributed. If cumulative losses exceed profits, it's reported as Accumulated Deficit (a negative balance), indicating historical underperformance.
留存收益代表公司自成立以来赚取的累计净利润，减去支付给股东的任何股息或发生的损失。它是资产负债表上股东权益的关键组成部分，显示了再投资于业务而非分配的收益部分。如果累计损失超过利润，则报告为累计亏损（负余额），表明历史表现不佳。

* **Accumulated Other Comprehensive Income (AOCI)**: Unrealized gains/losses (e.g., FX, securities).&#x20;
* **累计其他综合收益 (AOCI)**：未实现损益（如外汇、证券）。

Accumulated Other Comprehensive Income (AOCI) is a component of shareholders' equity on the balance sheet that tracks cumulative unrealized gains and losses from certain non-operating items not yet realized in net income. It acts as a holding account for items reported in other comprehensive income (OCI) on the statement of comprehensive income, preventing short-term volatility from affecting reported earnings.
累计其他综合收益 (AOCI) 是资产负债表上股东权益的一个组成部分，用于追踪某些尚未在净利润中实现的非经营项目的累计未实现损益。它充当综合收益表中报告的其他综合收益 (OCI) 项目的持有账户，防止短期波动影响报告收益。

* **Treasury Stock**: Repurchased shares, deducted at cost.
* **库存股**：回购的股份，按成本扣除。

Treasury stock refers to shares of a company's own stock that it has repurchased from shareholders and holds in its treasury, rather than retiring them. It's reported as a contra-equity account on the balance sheet, subtracting from total shareholders' equity (negative value), as it represents shares no longer outstanding.
库存股是指公司从股东手中回购并保存在金库中而不是注销的股票。它在资产负债表上作为权益抵减账户报告，从股东权益总额中减去（负值），因为它代表不再流通的股份。

| -------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |

## 7.4 **现金流量表 Cash Flow Statement**

### 7.4.1 什么是现金流量表？What Is the Statement of Cash Flows?

The statement of cash flows (also known as the cash flow statement) is a primary financial statement that provides information about a company's cash receipts and cash payments during an accounting period. It reconciles the beginning and ending cash balances on the balance sheet, classifying cash inflows and outflows into three categories: **operating activities** (core business cash), **investing activities** (asset acquisitions/disposals), and **financing activities** (funding sources/returns). Unlike the income statement, which uses accrual accounting, the cash flow statement focuses on actual cash movements, helping assess liquidity, solvency, and financial flexibility. It is a "flow" statement, reporting performance over time, and is required under US GAAP and IFRS.
现金流量表是一种主要的财务报表，提供有关公司在会计期间现金收入和现金支出的信息。它调节资产负债表上的期初和期末现金余额，将现金流入和流出分为三类：**经营活动**（核心业务现金）、**投资活动**（资产收购/处置）和**筹资活动**（资金来源/回报）。与使用权责发生制的损益表不同，现金流量表关注实际的现金流动，有助于评估流动性、偿债能力和财务灵活性。它是一个“流量”报表，报告一段时间内的表现，并且是 US GAAP 和 IFRS 要求的。

* **Cash Flows from Operating Activities**
* **经营活动产生的现金流量**

Cash flows from operating activities (CFO or operating cash flow) is the section of the cash flow statement that reports net cash generated or used by a company's core business operations during the period, such as selling products or providing services. It represents the cash version of the income statement's operating profit, excluding non-cash items and timing differences.
经营活动产生的现金流量（CFO 或经营现金流）是现金流量表的一部分，报告公司核心业务运营（如销售产品或提供服务）在此期间产生或使用的净现金。它代表了损益表营业利润的现金版本，排除了非现金项目和时间差异。

* **Cash Flows from Investing Activities**
* **投资活动产生的现金流量**

Cash flows from investing activities is the section of the cash flow statement that reports net cash used or generated from long-term asset transactions, such as buying or selling property, equipment, securities, or businesses, reflecting a company's capital allocation and growth strategies.
投资活动产生的现金流量是现金流量表的一部分，报告长期资产交易（如购买或出售房产、设备、证券或业务）使用或产生的净现金，反映了公司的资本配置和增长战略。

* **Cash Flows from Financing Activities**
* **筹资活动产生的现金流量**

Cash flows from financing activities is the section of the cash flow statement that reports net cash generated or used from transactions with creditors and owners, such as issuing or repaying debt, issuing or repurchasing stock, and paying dividends, reflecting how a company funds its operations and returns capital.
筹资活动产生的现金流量是现金流量表的一部分，报告与债权人和所有者交易（如发行或偿还债务、发行或回购股票、支付股息）产生或使用的净现金，反映了公司如何为其运营提供资金并回报资本。

| How to find operating/investing/financing cash flow in MooMoo: Selecting a company——公司——现金流量表<br />如何在 MooMoo 中查找经营/投资/筹资现金流：选择公司——公司——现金流量表 | ![](<images/入门考试学习资料 Learning Resources for Entry Knowledge Test-image-99.png>) | ![](<images/入门考试学习资料 Learning Resources for Entry Knowledge Test-image-100.png>) | ![](<images/入门考试学习资料 Learning Resources for Entry Knowledge Test-image-101.png>) |

## 7.5 **常见的财务指标 Common Financial Metrics**

### 7.5.1 **盈利比率 Profitability Ratios:**

1. **毛利率 Gross Margin:**
1. **毛利率 Gross Margin:**

Gross margin is a profitability ratio that measures the percentage of revenue remaining after subtracting the cost of goods sold (COGS), indicating how efficiently a company produces or acquires its products/services relative to sales.
毛利率是一个盈利能力比率，衡量扣除销货成本 (COGS) 后剩余收入的百分比，表明公司相对于销售额生产或获取其产品/服务的效率。

**How it's calculated:** (Revenue - COGS) / Revenue \* 100, often expressed as a percentage; sourced from the income statement, where higher margins suggest pricing power or cost control.
**计算方法：** (收入 - COGS) / 收入 \* 100，通常以百分比表示；源自损益表，较高的利润率表明具有定价权或成本控制能力。

**Impact:** Reflects core operational efficiency; expanding margins signal competitive advantages or scale, while declines may indicate rising costs, pricing pressure, or competition, influencing stock valuations and sector comparisons.
**影响：** 反映核心运营效率；扩大的利润率标志着竞争优势或规模，而下降可能表明成本上升、定价压力或竞争，从而影响股票估值和行业比较。

Example – Apple Inc. (AAPL): In FY2025, gross margin was around 47% (up YoY from services mix), highlighting premium pricing; analysts cite it as a buffer against hardware slowdowns, supporting AAPL's premium P/E ratio.
示例 – 苹果公司 (AAPL)：在 2025 财年，毛利率约为 47%（因服务组合同比上升），突显了溢价定价；分析师将其作为应对硬件放缓的缓冲，支持 AAPL 的高市盈率。

| How to check gross margin trend for an individual company in MooMoo: Select a company——公司——财务指标——毛利率<br />如何在 MooMoo 中查看单个公司的毛利率趋势：选择公司——公司——财务指标——毛利率 | ![](<images/入门考试学习资料 Learning Resources for Entry Knowledge Test-image-105.png>) |
| ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |

* **Net Profit Margin**
* **净利率**

Net profit margin is a profitability ratio that measures the percentage of revenue remaining as net income after all expenses, taxes, interest, and non-operating items are deducted, indicating overall efficiency in converting sales into profit.
净利率是一个盈利能力比率，衡量扣除所有费用、税款、利息和非经营项目后剩余净利润占收入的百分比，表明将销售转化为利润的整体效率。

**How it's calculated:** (Net Income / Revenue) \* 100, sourced from the income statement; it accounts for the bottom line after comprehensive costs.
**计算方法：** (净利润 / 收入) \* 100，源自损益表；它说明了扣除综合成本后的底线。

**Impact:** Reflects comprehensive cost management and tax efficiency; higher margins suggest strong profitability and resilience, while low ones may signal high overhead, debt burdens, or competitive pressures, influencing investor confidence and P/E ratios.
**影响：** 反映综合成本管理和税务效率；较高的利润率表明盈利能力强和具有弹性，而较低的利润率可能表明管理费用高、债务负担重或竞争压力大，从而影响投资者信心和市盈率。

Example – Alphabet Inc. (GOOGL): In FY2025, net profit margin was around 35% (up YoY from advertising strength), showcasing efficiency.
示例 – Alphabet Inc. (GOOGL)：在 2025 财年，净利率约为 35%（因广告业务强劲同比上升），展示了效率。

| How to check net profit margin trend for an individual company in MooMoo: Select a company——公司——利润表——财务详情——关键指标——归母净利率<br />如何在 MooMoo 中查看单个公司的净利率趋势：选择公司——公司——利润表——财务详情——关键指标——归母净利率 | ![](<images/入门考试学习资料 Learning Resources for Entry Knowledge Test-image-106.png>) |
| ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |

* **Return on Equity (ROE)**
* **净资产收益率 (ROE)**

Return on Equity (ROE) is a profitability ratio that measures how effectively a company generates profits from its shareholders' equity, indicating management efficiency in using investor capital.
净资产收益率 (ROE) 是一个盈利能力比率，衡量公司从股东权益中产生利润的有效性，表明管理层使用投资者资本的效率。

**How it's calculated:** (Net Income / Average Shareholders' Equity) \* 100, where average equity = (Beginning + Ending Equity) / 2; sourced from income statement and balance sheet.
**计算方法：** (净利润 / 平均股东权益) \* 100，其中平均权益 = (期初权益 + 期末权益) / 2；源自损益表和资产负债表。

**Impact:** Higher ROE suggests strong returns on investment and growth potential; low ROE may signal inefficiency, high debt, or poor profitability, influencing valuations like DuPont analysis.
**影响：** 较高的 ROE 表明投资回报率高且具有增长潜力；低 ROE 可能表明效率低下、债务高或盈利能力差，影响杜邦分析等估值。

Real US example – Apple Inc. (AAPL): In FY2025, ROE was around 171%, driven by high margins and buyback.
真实美国案例 – 苹果公司 (AAPL)：在 2025 财年，ROE 约为 171%，受高利润率和回购驱动。

To advertise company products<br />B. 宣传公司产品<br />C. To track employee performance<br />C. 追踪员工绩效 | **Correct answer: A**<br />**正确答案：A**<br />**Explanation:** Financial statements provide a structured overview of a company's financial activities, enabling stakeholders to assess its health. They are required by the SEC for public companies to ensure transparency and reduce information asymmetry.<br />**解释：** 财务报表提供了公司财务活动的结构化概览，使利益相关者能够评估其健康状况。SEC 要求上市公司提供财务报表，以确保透明度并减少信息不对称。 |

GAAP 和 IFRS<br />Correct answer: B | **Correct answer: B**<br />**正确答案：B**<br />**Explanation:** US GAAP is developed by the FASB and is required by the SEC for domestic public companies, emphasizing precision in the US legal environment.<br />**解释：** US GAAP 由 FASB 制定，SEC 要求国内上市公司遵守，强调在美国法律环境中的精确性。 |

Non-operating expenses<br />C. 营业外费用 | **Correct answer: B**<br />**正确答案：B**<br />**Explanation:** It's the top line, showing income from selling products or services, net of deductions like returns and allowances.<br />**解释：** 这是第一行，显示销售产品或服务的收入，扣除退货和折让等。 |

| --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------- |

| **5.What does Operating Income (EBIT) exclude?**<br />**5. 营业利润 (EBIT) 排除什么？**<br />A. Revenues and COGS<br />A. 收入和 COGS<br />B. Financing costs and taxes<br />B. 融资成本和税款<br />C. R\&D expenses<br />C. 研发费用 | **Correct answer: B**<br />**正确答案：B**<br />**Explanation:** EBIT is gross profit minus operating expenses, providing a view of operational performance without financing or tax effects.<br />**解释：** EBIT 是毛利减去营业费用，提供没有融资或税收影响的运营绩效视图。 |
| ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |

Gross profit plus non-operating income<br />B. 毛利加上营业外收入<br />C. Total assets minus liabilities<br />C. 总资产减去负债 | **Correct answer: A**<br />**正确答案：A**<br />**Explanation:** Net income is the bottom line, representing profit after all expenses, taxes, and adjustments, available to shareholders.<br />**解释：** 净利润是底线，代表扣除所有费用、税款和调整后可供股东分配的利润。 |
